It has just been really hard to manage this constant back-and-forth.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had hallucinations or strongly fixed beliefs that others said were not true when you were not in a high-energy or depressed mood episode? If so, did those symptoms last for at least 2 weeks on their own?\n\nPatient: No, I've never experienced anything like hallucinations or beliefs that weren't real, especially not when my mood was normal. My struggles have really just been about those extreme highs and lows. I haven't had those kinds of experiences at all.\n\nDoctor: During your high-energy periods, have you ever felt that way for at least 1 week or needed hospitalization, with it causing major problems at work, school, relationships, or leading you to do risky things? Or were those periods shorter and less severely impairing?\n\nPatient: Yes, these high-energy periods are definitely not short or mild, and they've been happening on and off for over a year. During these times, I experience extreme hyperactivity where I take on way too much work, become overly social, and have racing thoughts I just can't control. It absolutely causes major disruptions in my life rather than being a minor, short-lived phase.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, do you need much less sleep than usual and still feel rested?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, to be honest. I haven't noticed a significant change in my sleep patterns during those high-energy times.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, do you feel unusually confident or powerful, like you have special abilities or can do things others can’t?\n\nPatient: No, I don't feel like I have any special powers or feel unusually powerful. Even though I have a ton of energy and start way too many activities, I don't think I can do things that others can't.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, do you talk much more than usual or feel like you can’t stop talking?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure about that, to be honest. I haven't paid close attention to whether my talking habits change during those high-energy periods.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, are you much more easily distracted than usual, with your attention constantly pulled to unimportant things?"
  }
]
========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
To be honest, I'm not really sure about that. I haven't noticed my attention getting easily pulled away by unimportant things during those high-energy times.
